# Skills · S · part 334

809 entries, study-language to style. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **study-language**: Build a language-learning path with vocabulary, grammar, sentence mining, drills, reading and writing practice, and a spaced review schedule. Text-only; no audio generation or pronunciation scoring. ([fernandoleitepagani/StudySkills](https://github.com/fernandoleitepagani/StudySkills/tree/HEAD/skills/study/study-language))
- **study-later**: Bookmark a topic or subject for later study. Takes an optional prompt describing what to save; if omitted, infers the subject from the current conversation. Clarifies with the user when the subject is ambiguous. Saves the bookmark to .tutor/saved-topics.jsonl so it appears as a starting option the next time the user runs /tutor:study. ([patmagee/tutor](https://github.com/patmagee/tutor/tree/HEAD/skills/study-later) · ★8)
- **study-learn-slides**: Teach from a selected Codex Study material version and save traceable learning notes with verified page or section citations. ([Zuwei-Wang/codex-study](https://github.com/Zuwei-Wang/codex-study/tree/HEAD/plugins/codex-study/skills/study-learn-slides))
- **study-lecture**: Explain university lectures, slides, definitions, and derivations step by step using the supplied course material. Use when the student wants to understand theory, not when writing a report. ([ivanchetvergov/mcs-poly-ai-skills](https://github.com/ivanchetvergov/mcs-poly-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/study-lecture))
- **study-lecture-writer**: Create, revise, or continue rigorous learner-centered lectures, study notes, concept explanations, course notes, paper walkthroughs, mathematical appendices, experiment explanations, or technical tutorials. Use when the user asks to "write a lecture", "explain this concept", "turn this into notes", "make a learning path", "rewrite the lecture", "continue the next lecture", "save as Markdown", or asks follow-up questions showing a previous explanation missed definitions, derivations, examples, assumptions, or reader-level clarity. ([Kys75/study-lecture-writer](https://github.com/Kys75/study-lecture-writer/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **study-ledger**: Log goals, exams, results; answer how am I doing. ([tbaolong45/robo](https://github.com/tbaolong45/robo/tree/HEAD/hermes/skills/school/study-ledger) · ★1)
- **study-leitor-web**: Sessão de leitura no navegador para UM capítulo/seção por vez — Markdown (parser + tipografia de leitura própria), EPUB (epub.js) ou PDF (PDF.js), conforme o material. Progresso, destaques e notas ficam salvos incrementalmente em disco a cada evento. Self-shutdown por IDLE (sem heartbeat do navegador por N min) — não por grace fixo, porque uma sessão de leitura não tem fim garantido (o aluno pode fechar a aba a qualquer momento). Use quando o usuário quer ler no navegador em vez de no chat (estudo-fluxo-03-aprender). ([willianmendesf/study-agent](https://github.com/willianmendesf/study-agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/study-leitor-web))
- **study-library**: Initialize, inspect, search, and maintain an explicit local study library. Use for initializing a study library, finding notes or quizzes, checking links, listing due reviews, showing weak areas, and viewing the learner profile. Also trigger for Chinese requests such as 初始化学习库, 查找笔记, 检查链接, 到期复习, and 薄弱点. Do not use to author new study content or conduct a question-by-question spar session.
